\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}+z)\right\rvert\cdot\left\lvert\prod_{k\leq\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}}^{(i_{k})}\prod_{k>\alpha-\beta}W_{J}^{(i_{k})}\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}+z)\right\rvert\cdot\left\lvert\prod_{k\leq\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}}^{(i_{k})}\prod_{k>\alpha-\beta}W_{J}^{(i_{k})}\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}+z)\right\rvert\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\prod_{k\leq\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}}^{(i_{k})}\prod_{k>\alpha-\beta}W_{J}^{(i_{k})}\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}_{j}+z)\right\rvert\right]\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty},

where
\mathcal{B}=\{z:\lVert z\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{2\delta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}\}
, and
\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert
is the element-wise absolute operation. The fifth equality follows the independence between
W^{o}
and
\mathscr{W}_{\{j_{1},\dots,j_{\alpha-\beta}\}\cup J}
. We decompose the expectation term on the last line by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}_{j}+z)\right\rvert\right]

(39)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\begin{aligned} \sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}_{j}+z)\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},1}\end{aligned}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\begin{aligned} \sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}_{j}+z)\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},2}\end{aligned}\right],

where the value of
h>0
will be determined later, and

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\left\{\lVert W^{o}-\partial A_{r}\rVert_{\infty}\leq 12{\delta^{\prime}}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right\}},

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\left\{\lVert W^{o}-\partial A_{r}\rVert_{\infty}>12{\delta^{\prime}}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right\}}.

For the first term, based on Lemmas 6.1 and 6.2 of
Chernozhukov, Chetverikov and
Koike 2023
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\begin{aligned} \sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}+z)\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},1}\end{aligned}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{w\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(w+z)\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{1})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}}}\mathbb{P}\left[\lVert X_{J_{1}}-\partial A_{r-Y_{J_{2}}^{o}}\rVert_{\infty}\leq 12{\delta^{\prime}}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{1})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(12{\delta^{\prime}}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right)\right\},

for any
\gamma_{1}\in[0,1]
.
For the last term, based on Lemma 10.5 of
Lopes 2022
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\begin{aligned} \sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha}}\sup_{z\in\mathcal{B}}\left\lvert\nabla^{(i_{1},\dots,i_{\alpha})}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}+z)\right\rvert\cdot\mathbb{I}_{W^{o},2}\end{aligned}\right]\leq C\frac{1}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha}h}.

Plugging the last two results in
Eqs.

38
and
39
, the resulting upper bound for
T_{W_{J},1}(t)
is

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},1}(t)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\left[\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{1})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(12{\delta^{\prime}}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right)\right\}+\frac{1}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha}h}\right]

\displaystyle\times\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty},

for any
t\in[0,1]
,
\gamma_{1}\in[0,1]
and
h>0
.
Replacing the minimum with
1
and minimizing over
h>0
, we get

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},1}(t)\right\rvert\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{1})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Moreover, plugging-in

h=\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}{\delta^{\prime}}^{1+\gamma_{1}}}\sqrt{\frac{\lvert J_{1}\rvert}{(\log(ep))^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}+2}}},

we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},1}(t)\right\rvert\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{1})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}(\kappa_{J_{1}}(\delta^{o})+\kappa^{o}),

where
\delta^{o}=12\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(p\lvert J_{1}\rvert)}
and
\kappa^{o}=\frac{\delta^{\prime}\log(ep)}{\sigma_{\min}\sqrt{\lvert J_{1}\rvert}}
. In sum,

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},1}(t)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{1})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{1}}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}\min\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}(\delta^{o})+\kappa^{o}\},

for any
t\in[0,1]
,
\gamma_{1}\in[0,1]
as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
Now we bound

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},2}(t)\right\rvert=\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{\alpha}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}(W^{o}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+tW_{J}),(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rangle\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},2}\right]\right\rvert.

Conditional on
W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}
and
W_{J}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\lVert\nabla^{\alpha}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}\left(W^{o}+W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tW_{J}\right)\right\rVert_{1}\right|W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}},W_{J}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\lVert\nabla^{\alpha}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}\left(W^{o}+W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tW_{J}\right)\right\rVert_{1}\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W^{o}-\partial A_{r^{\prime}}\rVert_{\infty}\leq 10\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\}}\right|W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}},W_{J}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\lVert\nabla^{\alpha}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}\left(W^{o}+W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tW_{J}\right)\right\rVert_{1}\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W^{o}-\partial A_{r^{\prime}}\rVert_{\infty}>10\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\}}\right|W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}},W_{J}\right],

where
r^{\prime}=r-W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}-tW_{J}
is deterministic given
W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}
and
W_{J}
. Applying Lemmas 6.1, 6.2 of
Chernozhukov, Chetverikov and
Koike 2023
and Lemma 10.5 of
Lopes 2022
to the two terms, respectively,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\lVert\nabla^{\alpha}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta^{\prime}}\left(W^{o}+W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tW_{J}\right)\right\rVert_{1}\right|W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}},W_{J}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{2}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{2})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{2}}}\mathbb{P}\left[\left.\lVert W^{o}-\partial A_{r^{\prime}}\rVert_{\infty}\leq 10\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right|W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp},W_{J}\right]+C\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime\alpha}h}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{2}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{2})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{2}}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(10\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right)\right\}+C\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime\alpha}h},

almost surely, for any
\gamma_{2}\in[0,1]
and
h>0
. Putting the last two results together,

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},2}(t)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{2}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{2})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{2}}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(10\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right)\right\}+\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime\alpha}h}\right)

\displaystyle\times\mathbb{E}\left[\begin{aligned} \prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},2}\end{aligned}\right],

for any
\gamma_{2}\in[0,1]
and
h>0
.
Because
\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},2}=\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}\text{ or }\lVert W^{{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}\rVert_{\infty}\geq\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}\}}\geq\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}>\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}\}}+\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\rVert_{\infty}>\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}\}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}\mathbb{I}_{W_{J},2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}>\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}\}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}\mathbb{I}_{\{\lVert W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\rVert_{\infty}>\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\log(ep))^{\eta/2}}{\delta^{\prime\eta}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}(\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta}+\lVert W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta})\right],

for any
\eta>0
.
The resulting upper bound for
T_{W_{J},2}(t)
is

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},2}(t)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\left[\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{2}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{2})/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{2}}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(10\delta^{\prime}\sqrt{\log(ph)}\right)\right\}+\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime\alpha}h}\right]

\displaystyle\times\frac{(\log(ep))^{\eta/2}}{\delta^{\prime\eta}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}(\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta}+\lVert W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta})\right],

for any
\gamma_{2}\in[0,1]
,
\eta>0
and
h>0
. By similar choices of
h
with for
T_{W_{J},1}(t)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert T_{W_{J},2}(t)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\left[\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{2}}(\log(ep))^{(\alpha-\gamma_{2}+\eta)/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\gamma_{2}+\eta}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(\delta^{o}\right)+\kappa^{o}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\times\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}(\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta}+\lVert W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta})\right],

for any
t\in[0,1]
and
\eta>0
as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
Putting the upperbounds for
T_{W_{J},1}(t)
and
T_{W_{J},2}(t)
together, we obtain the following upper bound for the entire remainder term:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\beta-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{\alpha}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{J_{1}}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+Y_{J_{2}}+tW_{J}),(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rangle\right]dt\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\left[\frac{(\log(ep))^{\alpha/2}}{{\delta^{\prime}}^{\alpha}}\min\left\{1,\kappa_{J_{1}}\left(\delta^{o}\right)+\kappa^{o}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\times\left[\begin{aligned} &\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{1}}{\delta^{\prime}}^{\gamma_{1}}}{(\log(ep))^{\gamma_{1}/2}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert(\otimes_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}W_{j_{k}})\otimes W_{J}^{\otimes\beta}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}\\
&+\frac{\phi^{\gamma_{2}}{\delta^{\prime}}^{\gamma_{2}-\eta}}{(\log(ep))^{(\gamma_{2}-\eta)/2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{k=1}^{\alpha-\beta}\lVert W_{j_{k}}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\beta}(\lVert W_{J}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta}+\lVert W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\rVert_{\infty}^{\eta})\right]\end{aligned}\right],

for any
\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\in[0,1]
and
\eta>0
as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
This proves
Lemma

B.3
.

Lemma

B.3
implies all the remainder theorems in the main text and appendices of this paper. For example, in
Lemma

4.3
, one of the third-order remainder terms in
\mathfrak{R}^{(3,1)}_{W_{j}}
is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tW_{j}\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(X_{[1,j-1)}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+Y_{(j+1,n]}+tW_{j}\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right],

where
W_{[i,j]}^{\mathsf{C}}\equiv X_{[1,i)}+Y_{(j,n]}
and
W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\equiv X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1}
. Because of Assumption (
MIN-EV
),
\operatorname{Var}[Y_{(j+1,n]}|\mathscr{Y}_{(j+1,n]}]\succeq\underline{\sigma}^{2}\max\{n-j-3,0\}\cdot I_{p}
.
Let
\underline{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\equiv\underline{\sigma}^{2}\max\{j,0\}
and
\delta_{j}^{2}\equiv\sqrt{\delta^{2}+\underline{\sigma}_{j}^{2}}
.
Applying
Lemma

B.3
to this term with
\gamma_{1}=0
and
\gamma_{2}=\eta=q-3
, we obtain for
q\geq 3
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(X_{[1,j-1)}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+Y_{(j+1,n]}+tW_{j}\right),W_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-j}^{3}}\min\{1,\kappa_{[1,j-1)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j}\}

\displaystyle\times\left[\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert W_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}+\phi^{q-3}\mathbb{E}\left[\lVert W_{j}\rVert_{\infty}^{3}(\lVert W_{j}\rVert_{\infty}^{q-3}+\lVert X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1}\rVert_{\infty}^{q-3})\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-j}^{3}}\left[L_{3,j}+(2\phi)^{q-3}(\nu_{q,j-1}+\nu_{q,j}+\nu_{q,j+1})\right]\min\{1,\kappa_{[1,j-1)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j}\},

where
\delta^{o}_{n-j}\equiv 12\delta_{n-j}\sqrt{\log(pn)}
and
\kappa^{o}_{j}\equiv\frac{\delta_{n-j}\log(ep)}{\sigma_{\min}\sqrt{\max\{j,1\}}}
as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
Based on similar applications of
Lemma

B.3
to the other terms of
\mathfrak{R}^{(3,1)}_{X_{j}}
, for
q\geq 3
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,1)}\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-j}^{3}}\left(\sum_{j^{\prime}=j-3}^{j+3}L_{3,j^{\prime}}+(2\phi)^{q-3}\sum_{j^{\prime}=j-3}^{j+3}\nu_{q,j^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\kappa_{[1,j-3)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j},1\},

as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
Similar arguments and bounds apply to
\mathfrak{R}_{W}^{(3)}
and
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,2)}
.
For the fourth-order remainder terms, we consider the first term of
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,1)}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tX_{j}),X_{j}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,j-1)}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+Y_{(j+1,n]}+tX_{j}),X_{j}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt\right].

Applying
Lemma

B.3
to this term with
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{2}=1
and
\eta=q-4
, we obtain for
q\geq 4
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(X_{[1,j-1)}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+Y_{(j+1,n]}+tW_{j}\right),W_{j}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\phi\min\{1,\kappa_{[1,j-1)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j}\}

\displaystyle\times\left[\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-j}^{3}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert W_{j}^{\otimes 4}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{(\log(ep))^{(q-1)/2}}{(\delta_{n-j}/2)^{q-1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\lVert W_{j}\rVert_{\infty}^{4}(\lVert W_{j}\rVert_{\infty}^{q-4}+\lVert X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1}\rVert_{\infty}^{q-4})\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\phi\left[\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-j}^{3}}L_{4,j}+\frac{(\log(ep))^{(q-1)/2}}{(\delta_{n-j}/2)^{q-1}}(\nu_{q,j-1}+\nu_{q,j}+\nu_{q,j+1})\right]\min\{1,\kappa_{[1,j-1)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j}\},

as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
Based on similar applications of
Lemma

B.3
to the other terms of
\mathfrak{R}^{(4,1)}_{X_{j}}
, for
q\geq 4
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,1)}\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\phi\left(\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-j}^{3}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=j-4}^{j+4}L_{4,j^{\prime}}+\frac{(\log(ep))^{(q-1)/2}}{(\delta_{n-j}/2)^{q-1}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=j-4}^{j+4}\nu_{q,j^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\kappa_{[1,j-4)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j},1\},

as long as
\delta\geq\sigma_{\min}
and
\phi\delta\geq\frac{1}{\log(ep)}
.
Similar arguments and bounds apply to
\mathfrak{R}_{W}^{(4)}
and
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,2)}
.
Finally, for the sixth-order remainder terms, we consider the first term of
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{k}}^{(6,1)}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+tX_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes W_{k}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})+tX_{k},\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes W_{k}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right],

where
W^{\not\!\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}\equiv X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1}
. Applying
Lemma

B.3
to this term with
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{2}=1
and
\eta=q-3
, we obtain for
q\geq 4
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+tX_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes W_{k}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\phi\min\{1,\kappa_{[1,j-1)}(\delta_{n-j}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{j}\}

\displaystyle\times\left[\begin{aligned} &\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\delta_{n-k}^{3}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rvert\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert W_{k}^{\otimes 3}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}\\
&+\frac{(\log(ep))^{(q-1)/2}}{(\delta_{n-k}/2)^{q-1}}\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rvert\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\lVert W_{k}\rVert_{\infty}^{3}(\lVert W_{k}\rVert_{\infty}^{q-3}+\lVert X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1}\rVert_{\infty}^{q-3})\right]\end{aligned}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\phi L_{3,j}\left(L_{3,k}\frac{(\log(ep))^{5/2}}{\delta_{n-k}^{5}}+(\nu_{q,k-1}+\nu_{q,k}+\nu_{q,k+1})\frac{{(\log(ep))}^{(q+2)/2}}{(\delta_{n-k}/2)^{q+2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\kappa_{[1,k-1)}(\delta_{n-k-5}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{k},1\}.

Similarly, we get

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{k}}^{(6,1)}\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\phi L_{3,j}\left(\begin{aligned} \sum_{k^{\prime}=k-2}^{k+2}L_{3,k^{\prime}}\frac{(\log(ep))^{5/2}}{\delta_{n-k}^{5}}+\sum_{k^{\prime}=k-3}^{k+3}\nu_{q,k^{\prime}}\frac{{(\log(ep))}^{(q+2)/2}}{(\delta_{n-k}/2)^{q+2}}\end{aligned}\right)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\kappa_{[1,k-3)}(\delta_{n-k}^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{k},1\}.

Similar arguments and bounds apply to the other sixth-order remainder terms.

B.4
Proof of Anti-concentration Lemma

We use a similar approach with
Section

B.3
to upper bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{\{2,i-1\}},1}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]
. First, we decompose
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{\{2,i-1\}},1}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]
by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{\{2,i-1\}},1}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}),X_{\{2,i-1\}}\right\rangle dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}),\mathscr{X}_{\{2,i-1\}}\right\rangle\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)dt\right|X_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}),X_{\{2,i-1\}}\right\rangle\mathbb{I}_{>}(t)dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right],

where
\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)=\mathbb{I}\{\lVert X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}-\partial A_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}\rVert_{\infty}\leq\varepsilon^{o}\}
,
\mathbb{I}_{>}(t)=\mathbb{I}\{\lVert X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}-\partial A_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}\rVert_{\infty}>\varepsilon^{o}\}
.
Based on Lemma 2.3 of
Fang and Koike 2020b
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}),X_{\{2,i-1\}}\right\rangle\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\lVert\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}})\rVert_{1}\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}{\varepsilon}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

We use Tonelli’s theorem to switch the order between the integration and the expectation conditional on
\mathscr{X}_{(i-2,3)}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\mathbb{I}_{\leq}(t)\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}\right||\mathscr{X}_{(i-2,n+3)}\right]dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\left(\begin{aligned} &\mathbb{P}[X_{[3,i-2]}\in A_{r_{1},\varepsilon^{o}}|\mathscr{X}_{(i-2,n+3)}]\\
&+\mathbb{P}[X_{[3,i-2]}\in A_{r_{2},\varepsilon^{o}}|\mathscr{X}_{(i-2,n+3)}]\end{aligned}\right)\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{[3,i-2]}(\varepsilon^{o})\mathbb{E}[\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}]=\kappa_{[3,i-2]}(\varepsilon^{o})(\nu_{1}(X_{2})+\nu_{1}(X_{i-1})),

where
r_{1}=r-(1-t)X_{\{2,i-1\}}-X_{1}-X_{i}-(\delta+\varepsilon^{o})\mathbf{1}
and
r_{2}=r-(1-t)X_{\{2,i-1\}}-X_{1}-X_{i}+(\delta+\varepsilon^{o})\mathbf{1}
are Borel measurable functions with respect to
X_{(i-2,n+3)}
. On the other hand, based on Lemma 10.5 of
Lopes 2022
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}}),X_{\{2,i-1\}}\right\rangle\mathbb{I}_{>}(t)dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\lVert\nabla\varphi_{r,\delta+\varepsilon^{o}}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,i]}-tX_{\{2,i-1\}})\rVert_{1}\mathbb{I}_{>}(t)\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon h^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i-2,n+3)}\right]dt\right|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon h^{4}}\mathbb{E}[\lVert X_{\{2,i-1\}}\rVert_{\infty}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}]=\frac{1}{\varepsilon h^{4}}(\nu_{1,2}+\nu_{1,i-1}).

Putting the above result and
Eq.

21
back to
Eq.

20
, we get for any
h>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[X_{[1,i]}\in A_{r,\delta}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}]

\displaystyle\leq(\nu_{1,2}+\nu_{1,i-1})\left(C\frac{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}{\varepsilon}\max\left\{1,\kappa_{[3,i-2]}(\varepsilon^{o})\right\}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon h^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle+C\frac{\delta+2\varepsilon^{o}}{\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(ep)}{i-2}}+2\mu_{[3,i-2]}.

Plugging in
h=\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\frac{i-2}{(\log(ep))^{2}}}\right)^{1/4}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[X_{[1,i]}\in A_{r,\delta}|\mathscr{X}_{(i,n]}]

\displaystyle\leq C(\nu_{1,2}+\nu_{1,i-1})\frac{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}{\varepsilon}\max\left\{1,\kappa_{[3,i-2]}(\varepsilon^{o})+\kappa^{o}_{i-2}\right\}

\displaystyle+C\frac{\delta+2\varepsilon^{o}}{\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(ep)}{i-2}}+2\mu_{[3,i-2]},

where
\varepsilon^{o}=10\varepsilon\sqrt{\log(p\max\{i_{0}-2,1\})}
and
\kappa^{o}_{j}=\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(ep)}{\max\{j,1\}}}
, as long as
\varepsilon\geq\sigma_{\min}
.
Because the righthand side is not dependent on
r
, for any
\varepsilon\geq\sigma_{\min},\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\kappa_{[1,i]}(\delta)

\displaystyle\leq C\left((\nu_{1,2}+\nu_{1,i-1})\frac{\sqrt{\log(ep)}}{\varepsilon}\kappa_{[3,i-2]}(\varepsilon^{o})+\mu_{[3,i-2]}\right)

\displaystyle+C\frac{\delta+2\varepsilon^{o}}{\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(ep)}{i-2}}+C\frac{\overline{\nu}_{1,(1,i)}}{\sigma_{\min}}\frac{\log(ep)}{\sqrt{i-2}}.

B.5
Proof of Lemma
A.7

Suppose that
j,k\geq\frac{n}{2}
and
\alpha\leq 1
. Because
x\mapsto x^{\alpha}
is concave, by Jensen’s inequality, for any
q_{1},q_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{1},i+j}\overline{L}_{q_{2},(i,i+j)}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\overline{L}_{q_{1}}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{1},i+j}\overline{L}_{q_{2},(i,i+j)}}{\overline{L}_{q_{1}}}\right)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\overline{L}_{q_{1}}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{n(j-1)}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{1},i}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{2},i}}{\overline{L}_{q_{1}}}\right)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\overline{L}_{q_{1}}\left(\frac{n}{j-1}\overline{L}_{q_{2}}\right)^{\alpha}\leq 2^{\alpha}\overline{L}_{q_{1}}\overline{L}_{q_{2}}.

Similarly, for any
q_{1},q_{2},q_{3}>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{L_{q_{1},i+j}}{j-1}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}\overline{L}_{q_{3},i+(l,k+l)_{j-1}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{L_{q_{1},i+j}}{j-1}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{L_{q_{1},i+j}}{j-1}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}\overline{L}_{q_{3},i+(l,k+l)_{j-1}}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{L_{q_{1},i+j}}{j-1}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}}\right)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{1},i+j}\overline{L}_{q_{2},(i,i+j)}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{j-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{L_{q_{1},i+j}}{j-1}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}\overline{L}_{q_{3}}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{L_{q_{1},i+j}}{j-1}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}}\right)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{1},i+j}\overline{L}_{q_{2},(i,i+j)}\left(\frac{n}{j-1}\overline{L}_{q_{3}}\right)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{\alpha}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{q_{1},i+j}\overline{L}_{q_{2},(i,i+j)}\overline{L}_{q_{3}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\alpha+1}\overline{L}_{q_{1}}\overline{L}_{q_{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{j-1}L_{q_{2},i+[k+l]_{j-1}}\overline{L}_{q_{3}}^{\alpha}.

C
Details of the Taylor expansions for
m=1

C.1
Breaking the ring

We apply the Taylor expansion to
\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n]})
centered at
\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})
as follows:

\displaystyle\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n]})-\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}),X_{n}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}+tX_{n}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt.

Because
X_{[1,n)}
and
X_{n}
are dependent via
X_{1}
and
X_{n-1}
due to
1
-dependency, we re-apply the Taylor expansion to
\nabla\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})
and
\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})
centered at
X_{(1,n-1)}
:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}),X_{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt,\enskip\text{and}\enskip

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})\right\rangle~dt.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}\right\rangle~dt,\enskip\text{and}\enskip

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n-2}\right\rangle~dt.

In sum, because
\mathbb{E}[X_{j}]=0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n]})-\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}^{\otimes 2}]\right\rangle+\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}]\right\rangle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(3)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(3)}
is the summation of all the above third-order remainder terms. That is,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}+tX_{n}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n-2}\right\rangle~dt

To further decompose
\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(3)}
, we apply the Taylor expansion up to order
4
. For example,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}+tX_{n}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{6}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}+tX_{n}),X_{n}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt.

Again, to break the dependency between
\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})
and
X_{n}^{\otimes 3}
, we re-apply the Taylor expansion centered at
X_{1}+X_{n-1}
.

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})\right\rangle~dt.

Repeating this to the other third-order remainder terms, we get

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-3)}),X_{n-2}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-2)}),X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(3,n-1)}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}),X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\otimes(X_{n-1}+X_{n})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(4)},

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(4)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)}+tX_{n}),X_{n}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)}+t(X_{1}+X_{n-1})),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n-1})\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}+tX_{n-2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n-2}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}+tX_{n-2}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{n-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)}+tX_{2}),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)}+tX_{n-2}),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(3,n-1)}+tX_{3}),X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}\otimes X_{3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-2)}+t(X_{2}+X_{n-2})),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{1}\otimes(X_{2}+X_{n-2})\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-3)}+tX_{n-3}),X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n-2}\otimes X_{n-3}\right\rangle~dt.

The third-order moments in
Eq.

36
are further decomposed as in
Section

C.4
. As a result,

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(1,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle+\sum_{k=2}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6,1)}\right],

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-3)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-2}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(1,n-3)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-2}\otimes X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}]\right\rangle+\sum_{k=2}^{n-4}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6,2)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6,2)}\right],

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-2)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\otimes X_{1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(2,n-2)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\otimes X_{1}]\right\rangle+\sum_{k=3}^{n-3}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6,3)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6,3)}\right],

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(3,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(3,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes X_{2}]\right\rangle+\sum_{k=4}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6,4)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6,4)}\right],

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\otimes(X_{n-1}+X_{n})]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(1,n-2)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n-1}\otimes X_{n}\otimes(X_{n-1}+X_{n})]\right\rangle+\sum_{k=2}^{n-3}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6,5)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6,5)}\right],\enskip\text{and}\enskip

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes(X_{n-1}+X_{n})]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(2,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\otimes(X_{1}+X_{n})]\right\rangle+\sum_{k=3}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6,6)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6,6)}\right].

Because
Y
is Gaussian, by Lemma 6.2 in
Chernozhukov, Chetverikov and
Koike 2023
and Assumption (
VAR-EV
),

\displaystyle\left\lvert\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{(1,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{n}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{n^{3/2}}L_{3,n}\frac{(\log(ep))^{3/2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{n^{3/2}}L_{3,n}\frac{(\log(ep))^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}\sigma_{\min}}.

Putting all the terms together,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(3)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y}^{(3)}\right\rvert\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{n}}\overline{L}_{3}\frac{(\log(ep))^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}\sigma_{\min}}+\left\lvert\mathfrak{R}_{X}^{(4)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y}^{(4)}\right\rvert+\sum_{k=2}^{n-2}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X,X_{k}}^{(6)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X,Y_{k}}^{(6)}\right]\right\rvert,

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6)}=\frac{1}{6}\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6,1)}+\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6,2)}+\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6,3)}+\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6,4)}+\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6,5)}+\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{R}_{X,W_{k}}^{(6,6)}
.

C.2
First Lindeberg swapping under
1
-dependence

We apply Taylor’s expansion to each term with
\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}
of the Lindeberg swapping in
Eq.

15
. We only show the expansions for
j=3,\dots,n-2
here, but the calculations for
j=1,2,n-1
and
n
are similar. We recall the notations

W_{[i,j]}^{\mathsf{C}}\equiv X_{[1,i)}+Y_{(j,n]}\enskip\text{and}\enskip W_{[i,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}\equiv W_{[i-1,j+1]}^{\mathsf{C}}.

First,

\displaystyle\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+X_{j})

\displaystyle=\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]})+\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tX_{j}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt.

We further apply Taylor’s expansion to the second and third terms:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt,

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle~dt.

Last,

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tX_{j-2}\right),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tY_{j+2}\right),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes Y_{j+2})\right\rangle~dt.

In sum,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+X_{j})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\begin{aligned} &\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]})+\left\langle\nabla\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle\\
&+\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\right\rangle+\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,1)}\end{aligned}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]})\right]+\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j}\right]\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\right]\right\rangle+\mathbb{E}[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,1)}],

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tX_{j}\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tX_{j-2}\right),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tY_{j+2}\right),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes Y_{j+2})\right\rangle~dt.

\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(3,1)}
is similarly derived. For
j=1,2,n-1
and
n
,
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,1)}
is the same as
Eq.

40
but with zero in place of non-existing terms.
Summing over
j=1,\dots,n
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+X_{j})-\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+Y_{j})\right]=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,1)}\right]

To further decompose
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,1)}
, we apply the Taylor expansion up to order
4
. For example, where
j=4,\dots,n-3
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\varphi_{r}^{\varepsilon}\left(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tX_{j}\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{6}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tX_{j}),X_{j}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt.

Again, to break the dependency between
\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,n)})
and
X_{n}^{\otimes 3}
, we re-apply the Taylor expansion centered at
X_{1}+X_{n-1}
.

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}_{[j,j]}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}_{[j,j]}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})),X_{n}^{\otimes 3}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle~dt.

Repeating this to the other third-order remainder terms, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,1)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n]})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,j-2)}+Y_{(j+1,n]})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j-1}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}]+\mathbb{E}[X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j}^{\otimes 2}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,j-3)}+Y_{(j+1,n]})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j-2}\otimes X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(4,1)}\right],

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(4,1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{3}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(W^{\mathsf{C}}_{[j,j]}+tX_{j}),X_{j}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle~dt

(40)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tX_{j-2}\right),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j-2}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tY_{j+2}\right),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes Y_{j+2}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tX_{j-2}\right),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tY_{j+2}\right),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}^{\otimes 2}\otimes Y_{j+2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j-1,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-2}+Y_{j+2})\right),X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes X_{j-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j-1,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+t(X_{j-2}+Y_{j+2})\right),X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes Y_{j+2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j-1,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tX_{j-2}\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j-2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j+1]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tY_{j+2}\right),X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes Y_{j+2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j-2,j]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tX_{j-3}\right),X_{j}\otimes X_{j-1}\otimes X_{j-2}\otimes X_{j-3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(W_{[j,j+2]}^{{\perp\!\!\!\!\perp}}+tY_{j+3}\right),X_{j}\otimes Y_{j+1}\otimes Y_{j+2}\otimes Y_{j+3}\right\rangle~dt.

\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(4,1)}
is similarly derived. For
j=1,2,3,n-2,n-1
and
n
,
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,1)}
is the same as
Eq.

40
but with zero in place of proper terms.

C.3
Second Lindeberg swapping

For each
j=2,\dots,n-2
,

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j)}+X_{j}+Y_{(j,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j)}+Y_{(j,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle+\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,2,1)}

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,2,1)}=\int_{0}^{1}\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j)}+tX_{j}+Y_{(j,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}\rangle~dt
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,2,1)}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j)}+Y_{(j,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j)}+tX_{j}+Y_{(j,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j)}+tX_{j}+Y_{(j,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}\left(X_{(1,j-1)}+t(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})+Y_{(j+1,n-1)}\right),X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}\otimes(X_{j-1}+Y_{j+1})\right\rangle~dt.

Because
\mathbb{E}[X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j}]=\mathbb{E}[X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes Y_{j}]=0
,

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right]\right\rangle-\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(1,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=2}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,2,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(3,2,1)}\right]=\sum_{j=2}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(4,2,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(4,2,1)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(4,2,1)}
is the fourth-order remainder term above. Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(2,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\right]\right\rangle-\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(2,n-1)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{n}\otimes X_{1}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=3}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,2,2)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(3,2,2)}\right]=\sum_{j=3}^{n-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(4,2,2)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(4,2,2)}\right],\enskip\text{and}\enskip

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(1,n-2)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\right]\right\rangle-\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{2}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(1,n-2)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{n}\otimes X_{n-1}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=2}^{n-3}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(3,2,3)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(3,2,3)}\right]=\sum_{j=2}^{n-3}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j}}^{(4,2,3)}-\mathfrak{R}_{Y_{j}}^{(4,2,3)}\right].

We define
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,2)}=\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,2,1)}+\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,2,2)}+\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(3,2,3)}
and
\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,2)}=\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,2,1)}+\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,2,2)}+\mathfrak{R}_{W_{j}}^{(4,2,3)}
.

C.4
Third Lindeberg swapping

For
j=3,\dots,n-1
,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\Big\rangle.

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{j-2}\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+X_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right]

\displaystyle{$- \mathbb{E}\left[ \nabla^3 \rho_{r,\phi}^\varepsilon(X_{[1,k)} + Y_k + Y_{(k,j-1) \cup(j+1,n)}) \right],
\mathbb{E}[ X_j^{\otimes 3} ]
\Big\rangle.$}

We first consider the case with
6\leq j\leq n-1
.
For
3\leq k\leq j-4
, the Taylor expansion centered at
X_{[1,k)}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}
implies

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+X_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+tX_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt\right].

For the inner product terms with dependent factors, we repeat the Taylor expansion:

1.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{4}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}\otimes(X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big[\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+t(X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1})+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),

\displaystyle{$\mathbb{E}[X_j^{\otimes 3}] \otimes X_k \otimes(X_{k-1} + Y_{k+1})^{\otimes 2}
\Big\rangle dt \Big]$},

2.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}\left[X_{k}^{\otimes 2}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big[\int_{0}^{1}\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+t(X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1})+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),

\displaystyle{$\mathbb{E}[X_j^{\otimes 3}] \otimes X_k^{\otimes 2} \otimes(X_{k-1} + Y_{k+1})
\Big\rangle~dt \Big],$}

3.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}\otimes(X_{k-1}+Y_{k-1})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-2)}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[X_{k}\otimes X_{k-1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big[\int_{0}^{1}\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-2)}+tX_{k-2}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}\otimes X_{k-1}\otimes X_{k-2}\Big\rangle~dt\Big]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big[\int_{0}^{1}\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+tY_{k+2}+Y_{(k+2,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes X_{k}\otimes Y_{k+1}\otimes Y_{k+2}\Big\rangle~dt\Big].

For
\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+Y_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle
, the calculation is the same but with
Y_{k}
in place of
X_{k}
. By the second moment matching,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+X_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right]

\displaystyle{$- \mathbb{E}\left[ \nabla^3 \rho_{r,\phi}^\varepsilon(X_{[1,k)} + Y_k + Y_{(k,j-1) \cup(j+1,n)}) \right],
\mathbb{E}[ X_j^{\otimes 3} ]
\Big\rangle$}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-2)}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[X_{k}\otimes X_{k-1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+Y_{(k+2,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[Y_{k}\otimes Y_{k+1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{k}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{k}}^{(6,1)}\right],

where for
W_{k}=X_{k}
or
Y_{k}
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{k}}^{(6,1)}

(41)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{2}\left\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k)}+tW_{k}+Y_{(k,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\otimes W_{k}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+t(X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1})+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),

\displaystyle{$X_j^{\otimes 3} \otimes W_k \otimes(X_{k-1} + Y_{k+1})^{\otimes 2}
\Big\rangle dt$}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+t(X_{k-1}+Y_{k+1})+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),

\displaystyle{$X_j^{\otimes 3} \otimes W_k^{\otimes 2} \otimes(X_{k-1} + Y_{k+1})
\Big\rangle~dt$}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-2)}+tX_{k-2}+Y_{(k+1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\otimes W_{k}\otimes X_{k-1}\otimes X_{k-2}\Big\rangle~dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\Big\langle\nabla^{6}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,k-1)}+tY_{k+2}+Y_{(k+2,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)}),X_{j}^{\otimes 3}\otimes W_{k}\otimes Y_{k+1}\otimes Y_{k+2}\Big\rangle~dt.

For
k=1
,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{1}+Y_{(1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{1}+Y_{(1,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{(3,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[Y_{1}\otimes Y_{2}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{1}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{1}}^{(6,1)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{1}}^{(6,1)}
is the same as
Eq.

41
but with
Y_{n}
and
Y_{n-1}
in place of
X_{k-1}
and
X_{k-2}
, respectively.
For
k=2
,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{1}+X_{2}+Y_{(2,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{1}+Y_{2}+Y_{(2,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{(3,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[X_{2}\otimes X_{1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{(4,j-1)\cup(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[Y_{2}\otimes Y_{3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{2}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{2}}^{(6,1)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{2}}^{(6,1)}
is the same as
Eq.

41
but with
Y_{n}
in place of
X_{k-2}
.
For
k=j-3
,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-3)}+X_{j-3}+Y_{\{j-2\}\cup(j+1,n)})\right]

\displaystyle{$- \mathbb{E}\left[ \nabla^3 \rho_{r,\phi}^\varepsilon(X_{[1,j-3)} + Y_{j-3} + Y_{\lx@text@lbrace j-2\lx@text@rbrace \cup(j+1,n)}) \right],
\mathbb{E}[ X_j^{\otimes 3} ]
\Big\rangle$}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-5)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[X_{j-3}\otimes X_{j-4}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-4)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[Y_{j-3}\otimes Y_{j-2}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{j-3}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{j-3}}^{(6,1)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{2}}^{(6,1)}
is the same as
Eq.

41
but with
0
in place of
Y_{k+2}
.
For
k=j-2
,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-2)}+X_{j-2}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-2)}+Y_{j-2}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\nabla^{5}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-4)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\otimes\mathbb{E}[X_{j-2}\otimes X_{j-3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{j-2}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{j-2}}^{(6,1)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{2}}^{(6,1)}
is the same as
Eq.

41
but with
0
in place of
Y_{k+1}
and
Y_{k+2}
.
By the second moment matching,

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(X_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})-\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\varepsilon}(Y_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{j-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{k}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{k}}^{(6,1)}\right].

This is also the same for
j\in[1,5]
, where some terms in
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{k}}^{(6,1)}
are zero when appropriate.
In sum,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{[1,j-1)}+Y_{(j+1,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 3}]\right\rangle+\sum_{j=3}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{j-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{k}}^{(6,1)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{k}}^{(6,1)}\right].

Similarly,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(0\vee(j+2-n),j-2)}+Y_{(j+2,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j+1}]+\mathbb{E}[X_{j}\otimes X_{j+1}^{\otimes 2}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(0\vee(j+2-n),j-2)}+Y_{(j+2,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{\otimes 2}\otimes X_{j+1}]+\mathbb{E}[X_{j}\otimes X_{j+1}^{\otimes 2}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=4}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{j-3}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{k}}^{(6,2)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{k}}^{(6,2)}\right],\enskip\text{and}\enskip

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(X_{(0\vee(j+3-n),j-3)}+Y_{(j+3,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j}\otimes X_{j+1}\otimes X_{j+2}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla^{3}\rho_{r,\phi}^{\delta}(Y_{(0\vee(j+3-n),j-3)}+Y_{(j+3,n)})\right],\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j}\otimes X_{j+1}\otimes X_{j+2}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=5}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{j-4}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},X_{k}}^{(6,3)}-\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},Y_{k}}^{(6,3)}\right],

where
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{k}}^{(6,2)}
and
\mathfrak{R}_{X_{j},W_{k}}^{(6,3)}
are similarly derived as in
Eq.

41
.
